=== BUGGY CODE ===
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))

Observe the following faulty python code. It contains one or more bugs.
Fix the code and output only the fixed code.

```python
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

- Then do one small coupled-region scan: a directly connected guard, index update, return path, or call site that could still break the same fix.
- If the recheck is still off, refine the same local region before opening a distant new theory.
- Once the failing path and its directly coupled region look consistent, finalize the code.

Let's not rely on memory. The prompt code has DP with adjacency constraint (res1 += curr[0]) and uses max savings. It likely is intended for LeetCode 2646. Need fix bugs in code, not problem statement. Need identify bugs.

Let's inspect code:

```
class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for i, j in edges:
            g[i].append(j)
            g[j].append(i)
            
        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n
        
        def dfs(i, l, p:
            level[i] = l
            parent[i] = p
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    dfs(j, l + 1, i)
        
        undefinedLCA(a, b):
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a
        
        dfs(0, 0, -1)
        for i, j in trips:
            lca = LCA(i, j)
            path = []
            while i != lca:
                freq[i] += 1
                i = parent[i]
            freq[i] += 1
            while j != lca:
                freq[j] += 1
                j = parent[j]
        
        def dp(i, p):
            res0 = 0
            res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]
            for j in g[i]:
                if j != p:
                    curr = dp(j, i)
                    res0 += max(curr)
                    res1 += curr[0]
            return [res0, res1]
        ans = 0
        for i in range(n):
            ans += freq[i] * price[i]
        return ans - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Bugs:
1. Syntax: `def dfs(i, l, p:` missing closing parenthesis. Should be `def dfs(i, l, p):`
2. `undefinedLCA(a, b):` missing `def` and probably should be `LCA` or `lca`. Later called `LCA(i, j)`. Need define `def LCA(a, b):` or `def lca` and call. Also missing colon? Actually `undefinedLCA(a, b):` invalid. Fix to `def LCA(a, b):`.
3. In trip path counting, they mutate `i` and `j` loop variables. This is okay for loop variables but if using for i,j in trips, after first trip i changed, but next iteration reassigns. However inside they also use `i` and `j` for LCA? They call `lca = LCA(i, j)` before mutating. Fine. But if they use `path = []` unused. Fine. But there is a subtle bug: when counting path from i to lca, they increment freq[i] and then set i=parent[i]. At end after while, they increment freq[i] (lca). Then for j, while j != lca, increment and move. This increments lca once. Good. But if i == lca initially, first while skipped, freq[lca] +=1; second while counts j path. Good. If j == lca, second skipped, lca already counted. Good. If both? i==j==lca, counts lca once. Good.
4. LCA function: after leveling, if level[a] > level[b], swap a,b so b deeper. Then lift b. If a==b return. Then lift both. Good. But parent[0] = -1. If LCA called with root? fine. Need ensure no infinite loop if a,b not in same tree? tree connected. Good.
5. DP savings formula: `res1 = price[i] // 2 * freq[i]` is wrong for savings. It should be `(price[i] - price[i] // 2) * freq[i]` or `(price[i] + 1) // 2 * freq[i]` if half price is floor. Let's examine original code's ans - max(dp). If dp returns savings, res1 should be savings from halving current. Full contribution is `price[i] * freq[i]`. If halved price is `price[i] // 2`, savings = `price[i] * freq[i] - price[i]//2 * freq[i]` = `(price[i] - price[i]//2) * freq[i]` = `ceil(price[i]/2) * freq[i]`. The code uses `price[i] // 2 * freq[i]`, which is the halved cost, not savings. This is a bug. However, maybe they intended dp to return cost, not savings? Let's check res0 = 0, res1 = half cost, res0 += max(curr), res1 += curr[0], then ans - max(dp). If dp returns cost, res0 should be full cost of current + max? Let's derive:
If dp returns cost for subtree:
- current not halved: cost = price[i]*freq[i] + sum(min? or max? Wait if current not halved, children can be either, so choose min cost for each child: `price[i]*freq[i] + sum(min(curr))`.
- current halved: cost = price[i]//2*freq[i] + sum(not_half child) because child cannot halve: `curr[0]`.
Then answer = min(dp(root)) not ans - max. Code uses res0 = 0, res1 = half cost, res0 += max(curr), res1 += curr[0], return ans - max(dp). This is savings DP, but res1 is wrong. Also res0 should include current full? In savings DP, res0 = savings when current not halved: current savings 0 + child max. res1 = current savings + child not-halved savings. So yes res1 should be `(price[i] - price[i]//2) * freq[i]`. Then answer full - max(dp). Good.
But wait if price[i] is even, price[i]//2 = half, savings = price[i]//2. If odd, half price maybe floor, savings = ceil. The original problem maybe "half price" means integer division? Need know. LeetCode 2646 uses `price[i] / 2`? Let's recall constraints: price[i] is integer. In examples, price values even. If odd, how to compute? The statement likely says "make their price half of their original price" and since prices are integers, maybe "half" means `price[i] / 2`? In Python, if using integer division, half of 3 is 1? But "half" of 3 is 1.5 not integer. LeetCode usually uses integer? Let's recall solution code from memory:
```
def dfs(u, p):
    not_halve = price[u] * cnt[u]
    halve = price[u] // 2 * cnt[u]
    for v in adj[u]:
        if v == p: continue
        child_not_halve, child_halve = dfs(v, u)
        not_halve += min(child_not_halve, child_halve)
        halve += child_not_halve
    return not_halve, halve
return min(dfs(0, -1))
```
They use `price[u] // 2` as halved price. So if odd, half price is floor. Savings = price - price//2. Code in prompt's res1 should be `price[i] // 2 * freq[i]` if dp returns cost, but because they use savings, need adjust. Let's see if maybe they intended `res1` as "cost when halved" and `res0` as "savings when not halved"? No, ans - max(dp) would be weird. Let's test with even prices: If res1 = price//2 * freq = savings when even. For even prices, correct. For odd, wrong. The prompt's code maybe from a solution where prices are even? Not guaranteed. Need fix.

6. Another bug: In DP, `res0 += max(curr)` for not halved current. If curr is [savings_not_halved, savings_halved], max is correct because child can be either. `res1 += curr[0]` because if current halved, child cannot halved, so child not halved savings. Good. But if dp returns cost, res0 should use min and include current full. Let's decide which DP style to output. Need fixed code. We can either fix savings DP or convert to cost DP. Need ensure correct for odd prices and adjacency constraint.

Let's inspect prompt code's `ans - max(dp(0, -1))`. If dp returns savings, max savings. If dp returns cost, answer should be min(dp). The code's `res0 = 0` and `res0 += max(curr)` strongly indicates savings DP. The only issue is res1 formula. We can fix to `(price[i] - price[i] // 2) * freq[i]` or `(price[i] + 1) // 2 * freq[i]`. Need also maybe `max(dp(0,-1))` returns max savings. Good.

But wait if dp returns savings, res1 should be savings from halving current. If halved price is `price[i] // 2`, savings = `price[i] * freq[i] - price[i]//2 * freq[i]`. If price[i] is even, equals `price[i]//2 * freq[i]`. If odd, equals `(price[i]//2 + 1) * freq[i]`. The code uses `price[i] // 2 * freq[i]`, which is half cost. So fix.

7. Another bug: In trip counting, they use `path = []` unused. Not bug but can remove.

Let's produce final code:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a, b in edges:
            g[a].append(b)
            g[b].append(a)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            while a != c:
                freq[a] += 1
                a = parent[a]
            freq[a] += 1
            while b != c:
                freq[b] += 1
                b = parent[b]

        def dp(u: int, p: int):
            save0 = 0
            save1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v == p:
                    continue
                child = dp(v, u)
                save0 += max(child)
                save1 += child[0]
            return [save0, save1]

        total = sum(freq[i] * price[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Need check if `save1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]` is correct. If price[u] = 2, save=1. If price=10, save=5. If price=1, save=1. Good.
But wait if halved price is `price[u] // 2`, then savings = price - price//2. Yes.
Could also use `(price[u] + 1) // 2 * freq[u]`. Equivalent for integer. Use `price[u] - price[u] // 2` clearer.

Need consider if `max(child)` returns int but child list length 2. Good.

Potential issue: The DP returns savings for subtree, but `save0` for current not halved doesn't include current's own savings (0). Good.
Potential issue: In `save1`, if current halved, child cannot halve, so `child[0]` (child not halved savings). Good.
Potential issue: If current not halved, child can be either, so `max(child)`. Good.

Let's write final:

```
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a, b in edges:
            g[a].append(b)
            g[b].append(a)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def lca(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = lca(a, b)
            while a != c:
                freq[a] += 1
                a = parent[a]
            freq[a] += 1
            while b != c:
                freq[b] += 1
                b = parent[b]

        def dp(u: int, p: int):
            not_halved_saving = 0
            halved_saving = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v == p:
                    continue
                child = dp(v, u)
                not_halved_saving += max(child)
                halved_saving += child[0]
            return [not_halved_saving, halved_saving]

        total = sum(price[i] * freq[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

Potential issue: If price[u] = 0, savings = 0. Good.

    while j != lca:
        freq[j] += 1
        j = parent[j]
```
They mutate `i` and `j`. After first while, `i` is lca. They increment freq[i] (lca). Then second while uses `j` original? Wait `j` hasn't been mutated yet. Yes, `j` is still original endpoint. Good. So lca counted once. If they had mutated `j` before, no. Good.

```

    c = LCA(a, b)
    while a != c:
        freq[a] += 1
        a = parent[a]
    freq[a] += 1
    while b != c:
        freq[b] += 1
        b = parent[b]
```

=== RAW ANSWER ===
? We need output only fixed code. The final answer should be code block. Need ensure no extra text. Let's provide code block with fixed code.</think>```python
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a, b in edges:
            g[a].append(b)
            g[b].append(a)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def LCA(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = LCA(a, b)
            while a != c:
                freq[a] += 1
                a = parent[a]
            freq[a] += 1
            while b != c:
                freq[b] += 1
                b = parent[b]

        def dp(u: int, p: int):
            save0 = 0
            save1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v == p:
                    continue
                child = dp(v, u)
                save0 += max(child)
                save1 += child[0]
            return [save0, save1]

        total = sum(price[i] * freq[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List

class Solution:
    def minimumTotalPrice(self, n: int, edges: List[List[int]], price: List[int], trips: List[List[int]]) -> int:
        g = [[] for _ in range(n)]
        for a, b in edges:
            g[a].append(b)
            g[b].append(a)

        freq = [0] * n
        level = [0] * n
        parent = [0] * n

        def dfs(u: int, l: int, p: int) -> None:
            level[u] = l
            parent[u] = p
            for v in g[u]:
                if v != p:
                    dfs(v, l + 1, u)

        def LCA(a: int, b: int) -> int:
            if level[a] > level[b]:
                a, b = b, a
            d = level[b] - level[a]
            while d:
                b = parent[b]
                d -= 1
            if a == b:
                return a
            while a != b:
                a = parent[a]
                b = parent[b]
            return a

        dfs(0, 0, -1)

        for a, b in trips:
            c = LCA(a, b)
            while a != c:
                freq[a] += 1
                a = parent[a]
            freq[a] += 1
            while b != c:
                freq[b] += 1
                b = parent[b]

        def dp(u: int, p: int):
            save0 = 0
            save1 = (price[u] - price[u] // 2) * freq[u]
            for v in g[u]:
                if v == p:
                    continue
                child = dp(v, u)
                save0 += max(child)
                save1 += child[0]
            return [save0, save1]

        total = sum(price[i] * freq[i] for i in range(n))
        return total - max(dp(0, -1))
